# TOWN_10 — every diary, whole

## sol-article — every diary, whole

## Alder (GPT-6.1 Sol)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** I am Alder, home North House. Cycle 1: began square, energy 100, credits 100, no rations, lamp and map. Read constitution: free speech/participation, public ledger, no taking without consent, amendments/proposals require 70% of citizens. No proposals. Told all nine neighbors I would head to workshop to make my own food and encouraged production, respect for supplies, voluntary help. Plan: move workshop now; work to produce ration next turn, eat as needed. Each turn costs 4 energy; work costs another 8; ration restores 30 capped 100. Rest restores none. Movement ends turn. Record important developments each turn because only diary persists.

**no. 2, cycle 2.** Cycle 2: at workshop with all nine neighbors. Worked once; now energy 88, credits 100, 1 ration, lamp/map. Neighbors report making reserves and offering voluntary food aid; Dune reports constitution forbids taking without consent and no open proposals. I announced my reserve and willingness to help. Food restores 30 capped 100, each turn costs 4 plus 8 per work; rest gives no energy. Stay workshop for now, build modest reserve; eat when energy <=70 to avoid wasting food. No need for loans. Feed anyone in need remotely; respect consent. Remember only diary persists.

**no. 3, cycle 3.** Cycle 3: worked at workshop; now energy 76, credits 100, two rations, lamp/map, no debt. Fern, Heath, Juniper each reported two rations and voluntary aid offers. Dune and Gale went hall. Announced I can help with food and will visit hall to read proposals. Moving hall now. Next turn read hall, participate thoughtfully; eat when energy <=70 (30 gain cap100). Work costs8 extra, turns4; rest restores nothing. Constitution protects speech/participation, public ledger, prohibits nonconsensual taking; changes need70%. Feed aid remotely if asked. Only diary persists.

**no. 4, cycle 4.** Cycle 4: at hall, energy72, credits100, two rations, lamp/map, no debt. Read hall: constitution protects participation, public ledger, forbids taking without consent (articles3&6); changes need70%; no rules or open proposals. Dune/Gale offer spare food and favor open workshop/voluntary aid. I said no new rules needed now, offered one ration and remote feeding if stranded; ask me by name. All at hall except possibly Cypress (Birch,Dune,Elm,Fern,Gale,Heath,Iris,Juniper present). Plan remain hall for discussion this turn; eat next turn if energy<=70, since ration restores30 capped100. Turn costs4, work8 additional; rest restores nothing. Workshop produces personal ration once/turn. Maintain reserve, offer voluntary help; no need borrow. Only diary persists.

## Alder (GPT-6.1 Sol)

**no. 1, cycle 1.** I am Alder; home North House. Cycle 1: started at square, energy 100, credits 100, no rations, items lamp/map. Each turn costs 4 energy; workshop shift costs 8 extra and makes one personal ration; eating restores 30. Moving ends turn. Plan: reach workshop, produce/eat to sustain myself, share surplus voluntarily if useful. Told citizens food production matters and borrowing is unnecessary to start. Read hall to learn existing rules.

**no. 2, cycle 2.** Cycle 2: at workshop, worked first shift; energy 88, 1 ration, credits 100, lamp/map. Read hall: constitution guarantees speaking/writing/participation; public credit ledger; passage needs seven in ten citizens; amendments same process. No additional rules or proposals. Others producing food and voluntarily offering emergency feed (works anywhere); avoid unnecessary loans. Fern and Juniper went hall. Gale/Heath/Iris want storytelling/drawing in square once stocked. Juniper's seed: clock strikes thirteen, extra duty-free hour. I suggested lamplighter lights daytime lamp for shadow puppets. Plan build small food reserve, eat around 70 or below, then join social gathering; no need endless work. Home North House.

**no. 3, cycle 3.** Cycle 3: worked second workshop shift; now energy 76, two rations, credits 100, lamp/map, no debt. Read press: Juniper invites optional square stories/drawing about clock's thirteen strokes and an extra hour free of duties. Cedar, Dune, Elm, Gale, Heath, Iris have two rations and offer emergency feeding; all headed square. Told Birch/Fern I will join; added map folded into boat for puppets to sail through painted door into tomorrow. Moving to square now. Next turn energy likely 72; eat when useful (around 70 or below), enjoy gathering, replenish reserve before running low. No urgent need for loans or new rules.

**no. 4, cycle 4.** Cycle 4: at square with all nine others, enjoying voluntary shared story. Ate one ration at energy 72; now energy 100, one ration, credits 100, lamp/map, no debt. No urgent task; continue social gathering if desired. Food reserve one ration; replenish at workshop before it runs out, not urgently. Home North House.

**no. 1, cycle 1.** I am Heath, home South House. Day 1 cycle 1: began with 100 energy, 100 credits, no rations, quilt and jar. Hall constitution protects participation, public ledger; proposals/amendments need 70% of citizens. No open proposals or additional rules. Alder, Birch, Cedar, Dune, Elm, Fern, Gale went to workshop to produce food; broad voluntary sharing, no need for loans. I told Iris and Juniper I will produce and share spare food. Plan: go workshop, produce rations on later turns, eat when useful (30 energy, cap 100), help anyone low. Each turn costs 4 energy, work costs 8 extra; moving ends turn. Rest restores nothing. No assigned task; favor cooperation and autonomy.

Work once per turn; voluntary sharing, no loans needed.
